## q02 · Hollow Knight 的配乐怎么样？

How is the soundtrack in Hollow Knight?

**English answer / 英文回答**

(9/10) ​Hollow Knight is a breathtaking journey through a beautifully dark and tragic world. The gameplay is surgical perfection. Tight, responsive controls, the hypnotic satisfaction of nail-pogoing, and boss fights that demand absolute respect and rhythm. You get lost, you adapt, you overcome.  An untouchable monument of the Metroidvania genre and an absolute work of art. 10/10.

## q04 · Monster Hunter Wilds 运行流畅吗，还是存在帧率问题？

Does Monster Hunter Wilds run smoothly, or is the frame rate a problem?

**English answer / 英文回答**
Monster Hunter Wilds' frame rate remains uneven across setups: reported results include 50–60 FPS on a low-end PC with drops in the Plains, and around 50 FPS with balanced DLSS and no frame generation. Smooth laptop play and improvements after fixes are also reported, although some setups still rely on frame generation.

**Chinese answer / 中文回答**
Monster Hunter Wilds 的帧率表现因配置而异：低配置 PC 有达到 50–60 FPS、但在平原区域掉帧的记录，平衡模式 DLSS 且不开帧生成时也有约 50 FPS 的反馈。修复后改善和笔记本流畅运行的体验同样存在，不过部分配置仍依赖帧生成。

The dificulties of high star monsters is okay not really hard at first and pretty easy when you already familiar with the monster pattern and behaviour. hope they increase their dificulties in Ascendences  overall this game really addictive and i personally recomended this game to everyone who wants to play action games

## q12 · 长期粉丝如何评价 Warhammer 40,000: Dawn of War III 的玩法变化？

What do longtime fans think of the gameplay changes in Warhammer 40,000: Dawn of War III?

**English answer / 英文回答**
Compared with earlier Dawn of War games, Dawn of War III draws criticism for MOBA-like maps, smaller battles and the effort of managing heroes and creeps, leaving its mixture of designs unsatisfying. Base-building fares better in one comparison, improving on Dawn of War II while remaining less substantial than the original game.

**Chinese answer / 中文回答**
与系列前作相比，Dawn of War III 的 MOBA 式地图、缩小的战斗规模，以及同时管理英雄和小兵的负担，是玩法变化中最受批评的部分。基地建设则有相对积极的评价：比 Dawn of War II 有进步，但仍不如初代完整。

**Review note / 核验提示**
将观点归属于长期粉丝时，需要核对评论者背景；这份草稿没有采用明确表示自己是新玩家的评论。
Check reviewer background when attributing opinions to longtime fans; the explicitly new player is not used in this draft.

**Sentence evidence / 逐句证据：**

- c1 中文：与系列前作相比，Dawn of War III 的 MOBA 式地图、缩小的战斗规模，以及同时管理英雄和小兵的负担，是玩法变化中最受批评的部分。
  - English: Compared with earlier Dawn of War games, Dawn of War III draws criticism for MOBA-like maps, smaller battles and the effort of managing heroes and creeps, leaving its mixture of designs unsatisfying.
  - `a962` / `steam:285190:236999842:chunk:001` (Warhammer 40,000: Dawn of War III) · 有用投票 / helpful votes: 0: I will dig in my heels &amp; defend the voice acting, music &amp; art style. It&#x27;s a 5/5 on atmosphere, it is a true WH40K game. But at the end of the day, gameplay rules with an iron fist &amp; trying to merge the 2 previous games constitutions was poorly executed, if it was possible in the first place.  If you want good feels, this knocked it out of the park. But if you want a good game? only if you&#x27;re ok with overpaying...
  - `a170` / `steam:285190:214131511:chunk:001` (Warhammer 40,000: Dawn of War III) · 高赞 / highly upvoted · 有用投票 / helpful votes: 13: Played the campaign. Played a bunch of skirmish matches. Got wrecked in Multiplayer.  Things I like: - Animations and graphics - Unit abilities - There is some basic base building  Things I didn&#x27;t like: - No horde survival mode - Small scale battles for a 40k title - Maps feel like they were trying to make this into a MOBA - Really wanted all races, even if it was DLC  Bottom line... I reinstalled DOW Soulstorm, added the Ultimate apocalypse mod and carried on from there. Much better.

## q42 · Monster Hunter Wilds 的画面值得付出性能代价吗？

Are the graphics of Monster Hunter Wilds worth the performance cost?

**English answer / 英文回答**
Monster Hunter Wilds' visuals do not have a consistently acceptable performance trade-off: attractive graphics coexist with smooth, crash-free play on some setups and reliance on frame generation on others. Around 50 FPS with balanced DLSS and no frame generation is considered acceptable in one account, though better performance is still wanted. Other feedback finds the performance poor and prefers Monster Hunter World's graphics and presentation.

**Chinese answer / 中文回答**
Monster Hunter Wilds 的画面与性能取舍并没有统一答案：同样是认可画面，有的配置体验流畅、没有崩溃，有的仍需依赖帧生成。平衡模式 DLSS、不开帧生成时约 50 FPS 的表现，有体验认为可以接受，但仍希望进一步改善。另一些反馈则对性能不满，也更喜欢 Monster Hunter World 的画面与整体呈现。

**Sentence evidence / 逐句证据：**

- c1 中文：Monster Hunter Wilds 的画面与性能取舍并没有统一答案：同样是认可画面，有的配置体验流畅、没有崩溃，有的仍需依赖帧生成。
  - English: Monster Hunter Wilds' visuals do not have a consistently acceptable performance trade-off: attractive graphics coexist with smooth, crash-free play on some setups and reliance on frame generation on others.
  - `a134` / `steam:2246340:236655344:chunk:001` (Monster Hunter Wilds) · 有用投票 / helpful votes: 0: Wilds might be one of my top games of all time. Again I&#x27;m not a long time fan of the franchise, but from the games I have played, Wilds just feels like the most polished vision of Monster Hunter while working within the confines of the RE Engine.  Graphically, it&#x27;s beautiful, and I&#x27;m very aware that I&#x27;m fortunate enough to have experienced the game after a year of patches and QoL changes, even now I have to use frame generation to sit comfortably in the triple digit FPS range.  Despite that, I really liked the story with the Guardian monsters, and just subtle elements like bows having infinite arrow coatings was really nice as a bow main. That said, wasn&#x27;t a fan of the insect glaive&#x27;s handling of the kinsect gauges.  Very excited for Ascendance.

## q43 · 哪些游戏 Bug 很多，甚至经常崩溃？

**English answer / 英文回答**
RollerCoaster Tycoon World and Spacebase DF-9 are clear examples in the retrieved crash reports, with construction crashes in the former and a session becoming broken after 13 hours in the latter. Crashes also appear in accounts of Youngblood, Warzone after an update, and Expedition 33 while loading the second cutscene on departure.

在检索到的崩溃反馈中，RollerCoaster Tycoon World 和 Spacebase DF-9 尤其明显：前者会在建造时崩溃，后者有游玩 13 小时后崩溃、无法正常继续的记录。Youngblood、更新后的 Warzone，以及 Expedition 33 出发时加载第二段过场动画，也有崩溃反馈。

## q46 · 哪些游戏即使在较好硬件上也优化差、帧率低？

Which games have poor optimization and low FPS even on good hardware?

**English answer / 英文回答**
RollerCoaster Tycoon World is a strong example of weak performance despite capable hardware: large parks lag even on good systems, and a laptop above the recommended specifications reports 20–55 FPS in small parks. Solar Crown also has a report of failing to reach 60 FPS on an RX 7900 XT. Warzone, Redfall and Red Dead Redemption 2 receive additional optimization criticism, although those accounts offer less specific hardware-and-FPS comparisons.

**Chinese answer / 中文回答**
RollerCoaster Tycoon World 是硬件较好仍然表现不佳的明显例子：大型公园会卡顿，超过推荐配置的笔记本运行小型公园时，也有 20–55 FPS 的记录。Solar Crown 则有 RX 7900 XT 仍无法达到 60 FPS 的反馈。Warzone、Redfall 和 Red Dead Redemption 2 也受到优化方面的批评，不过相关记录缺少同样具体的硬件与帧率对照。

**Review note / 核验提示**
RCT World 和 Solar Crown 的硬件与低帧率证据最具体；其他游戏的优化批评缺乏同样可比的硬件信息。

## q56 · Hollow Knight 对新玩家来说有多难？

## q81 · 玩家不喜欢 Test Drive Unlimited Solar Crown 驾驶和成长系统的哪些方面？

What do players dislike about the driving and progression in Test Drive Unlimited Solar Crown?

**English answer / 英文回答**
Solar Crown frustrates players with weak handling and slow progression. Level-gated unlocks and repetitive early races make earning cars and customization time-consuming, while stock-car requirements reduce the value of upgrades. ## q89 · Cyberpunk 2077 终于修好了吗：运行良好、Bug 都消失了吗？

Is Cyberpunk 2077 finally fixed: does it run well, and are the bugs gone?

**English answer / 英文回答**
Cyberpunk 2077 is not uniformly fixed across the reported setups: minimal issues after a late purchase coexist with an account of failing to run the game even at the lowest settings.

**Chinese answer / 中文回答**
从这些配置体验来看，Cyberpunk 2077 尚不能被概括为已经完全修好：既有较晚购买后几乎没遇到问题的体验，也有最低设置下仍无法运行的情况。

**Review note / 核验提示**
较晚购买后问题很少，只是一位玩家的体验，不能证明所有配置上的游戏问题都已修好。
Minimal issues after a later purchase is an individual experience, not proof of a universally fixed version.

## q91 · Resident Evil Resistance 平衡吗，是否没有技术问题？

Is Resident Evil Resistance balanced, and does it work without technical problems?

**English answer / 英文回答**
Resident Evil Resistance's balance is criticized as uneven between survivors and masterminds, with long matchmaking waits and a diminished population adding to the friction. A highly upvoted review describes door-blocking zombie exploits and cheating programs that disrupt play. Successful coordinated escapes can still be fun, but do not remove the imbalance or repetition.

## q93 · Test Drive Unlimited Solar Crown 的驾驶操作和帧率如何？

How do the driving controls and frame rate feel in Test Drive Unlimited Solar Crown?

**English answer / 英文回答**
Solar Crown's driving is divisive, with criticism of handling alongside enjoyment of early cars and the different feel of older vehicles. Performance reports include an RTX 3070/Ryzen 5 5600X setup initially unable to hold 60 FPS at 1080p with minimum settings and heavy DLSS, followed by substantial improvement in a February 2026 edit. Other experiences remain poor, including a drop to five frames after fast travel.

**Review note / 核验提示**
请保留早期车辆、2026 年 2 月更新等条件；不要把不同玩家的报告合并成同一套硬件体验。

## q94 · Clair Obscur: Expedition 33 的剧情和战斗相比如何？

How do the story and combat of Clair Obscur: Expedition 33 compare?

**English answer / 英文回答**
Expedition 33's emotional storytelling is a major strength, although a slower middle section and waning interest around Act 3 temper that praise. Its distinctive turn-based combat with real-time parrying and dodging is similarly divisive: rewarding challenge for some becomes exhausting timing pressure for others.

## q96 · Call of Duty: Warzone 玩起来稳定、流畅吗？

Is Call of Duty: Warzone stable and smooth to play?

**English answer / 英文回答**
Warzone's smoothness is undermined in the reported experience by lag, packet bursts, desynchronization, disconnects and updates that introduce problems. A separate 65–75 FPS report at maximum settings without upscaling shows that performance can differ, rather than establishing consistent stability.

## q100 · 哪些游戏优化好、运行流畅？

Which games are well optimized and run smoothly?

**English answer / 英文回答**
Resident Evil Requiem and Left 4 Dead 2 receive optimization praise, with Requiem accounts including smooth 1440p play on a five-year-old PC and performance above 100 FPS. Cyberpunk 2077 has smooth-play reports on Linux and a full AMD setup, while Monster Hunter Wilds receives positive feedback after an optimization update. Red Dead Redemption 2 without frame generation and GTA V Legacy provide further examples of smooth PC play.

**Chinese answer / 中文回答**
Resident Evil Requiem 和 Left 4 Dead 2 都因优化受到肯定，前者有使用五年的 PC 流畅运行 1440p，以及帧率超过 100 FPS 的记录。Cyberpunk 2077 在 Linux 和全 AMD 配置上有流畅运行的反馈，Monster Hunter Wilds 则有优化更新后表现良好的体验。Red Dead Redemption 2 不依赖帧生成的流畅表现，以及 GTA V Legacy 的 PC 体验，也提供了其他例子。
